## `AGO-0149-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Purity Analyst
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Honey Antibiotic Residue & HMF Content Lab
- **Intro:** Purity Analyst — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Honey Antibiotic Residue & HMF Content Lab — Verify Laboratory Environmental Conditions: Temperature: 20±2°C; Relative Humidity: 50±10% RH; Airborne particle count: ≤ 352,000 particles/m³ (ISO 8 class) for particles ≥ 0.5 µm.
- **Key tolerance:** Temperature: 20±2°C; Relative Humidity: 50±10% RH; Airborne particle count: ≤ 352,000 particles/m³ (ISO 8 class) for particles ≥ 0.5 µm.
- **Standards:** `ISO/IEC 17025:2017`, `Method 998.12`, `Regulation 37`, `NB 1234:2018`
- **Detail pages:** [EN](https://sop.go2abroad.space/en/sop/AGO-0149-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [中文](https://sop.go2abroad.space/zh/sop/AGO-0149-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [DE](https://sop.go2abroad.space/de/sop/AGO-0149-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [日本語](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ja/sop/AGO-0149-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [FR](https://sop.go2abroad.space/fr/sop/AGO-0149-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [ES](https://sop.go2abroad.space/es/sop/AGO-0149-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [PT](https://sop.go2abroad.space/pt/sop/AGO-0149-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [IT](https://sop.go2abroad.space/it/sop/AGO-0149-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [한국어](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ko/sop/AGO-0149-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [العربية](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ar/sop/AGO-0149-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [हिन्दी](https://sop.go2abroad.space/hi/sop/AGO-0149-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html)
